# About this item

*    Complete Set: Conquer a variety of nut removal tasks effortlessly with this bot extractor kit. Our bolt extractor set includes 33 of the most commonly used and needed bolt extractors, covering sizes from 6mm to 3/4". It is suitable for tasks like automotive repairs, home renovations, dismantling auto parts, and machine maintenance. 
*    Versatile Tool Compatibility: Our extractor set is equipped with adapters and square head sockets that fit 3/8" and 1/4" manual or power tools, such as adjustable wrenches, ratchets, and electric wrenches. You can perform disassembly tasks using the tools you already have without the need for additional accessories or tools. 
*    Cr-Mo Bolt Extractors: The broken bolt extractors are made from high-hardness, robust Cr-Mo steel material, and they are resistant to breakage. The sandblasted surface enhances wear resistance and rust resistance. The extractors are laser-engraved with sizes for easy identification and quick selection. 
*    Non-Slip Design: The stripped bolt remover is designed with a reverse spiral for a secure grip on threads, especially when counterclockwise disassembling, ensuring a tight connection. The hexagonal top design prevents wrenches or sockets from slipping off, making bolt removal efficient and effortless. 
*    Waterproof Storage Case: The nut extractor set comes with a waterproof PP case, providing organized storage for your tools. It's easy to carry anywhere and ensures your tools are neatly stored, preventing them from getting lost, rusting, or getting damaged.

## Product description

# Effortless Removal of Rusty Stuck Nuts

![Image 74: bolt extractor kit](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

Our 29-piece multi-functional bolt extractor set is perfect for home renovations, auto repairs, and DIY projects. It allows you to effortlessly remove stuck or rusty nuts and bolts. Crafted from high-hardness steel, it offers a strong grip without breaking or deforming.

![Image 76: screw extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

You can use our bolt extractor kit with an adjustable wrench or power tools for disassembly. When striking or screwing nuts, tiny metal fragments may scatter, so we recommend wearing safety goggles and gloves for secure operation.

![Image 78: extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The bolt extractors come in various sizes and are made of high-quality, high-hardness chromium-molybdenum steel (Cr-Mo). They provide exceptional durability and resistance to breakage, even under heavy force.

![Image 80: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

Our stripped bolt remover is compatible with 3/8" and 1/4" manual or power tools, such as adjustable wrenches, ratchets, and electric wrenches. The reverse thread design ensures secure removal of rusty or tight nuts, reducing slippage.

![Image 82: bolt extractor](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The waterproof case included in the extractor set holds all 29 pieces securely. It's ideal for organizing and carrying your tools, preventing them from rusting or damaging.

![Image 84: stripped bolt remover](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find this bolt extraction set to be a best tool they've ever bought, with good functionality and value for money. They appreciate its ease of use, with one customer noting how it makes stuck bolts easy to remove, and find it really comes in handy for various tasks. The durability receives mixed feedback.

Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. I took my VW in to have the differential lube changed and was told that the Allen drive plug was too rusty and they couldn't do the job. When I checked the plug, it was evident that they tried to remove it and stripped the Allen drive socket in the plug. I bought the Vevor Screw Extractor set and found the correct size. I tapped the extractor into the stripped plug then connected the ratchet, applied some force and Presto the plug began to unscrew!

I checked the screw extractor afterwards and it had no sign of wear. If you need to remove a stubborn fastener, I would give these a try! Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. Let me start with this kit only did half of the work it should have done. The extractor pieces are great and do their job well. However the left handed drill bits are terrible. They might be titanium, maybe. They are not cobalt though for sure. They wouldnt even hardly drill into a bolt I snapped the head off on my engine block. However, harbor freights left hand drill bits did the job in only a few seconds. Their cobalt left hand drill bits are the real deal except that cost me almost as much as this kit did. So I wouldnt invest in this kit if you need to get it done right, you can just buy the extractor set without the bits and go find the drill bits somewhere else. These drill bits might be good enough for wood, but that's about it. # Product Summary: VEVOR Bolt Extractor Set, 32-Piece Bolt and Nut Remover Set, 6mm to 10mm, 13/32" to 3/4", CR-MO Steel Extraction Socket Set with Storage Case, for Removing Damaged Rusted Bolts, Nuts and Screws

From VEVOR

4.6 out of 5 stars, 402 ratings

[Customer reviews](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0CKQKFLJL?ref=dp_product_quick_view#customer-reviews_feature_div)
50+ bought in past week

## Price

One-time purchase: $43.90

[Purchasing options](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0CKQKFLJL?ref=dp_product_quick_view#buybox)

[Other sellers](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0CKQKFLJL?ref=dp_product_quick_view#dynamic-aod-ingress-box)

[Other sellers](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0CKQKFLJL?ref=dp_product_quick_view#unqualifiedBuyBox)

## About this Item

*   Complete Set: Conquer a variety of nut removal tasks effortlessly with this bot extractor kit. Our bolt extractor set includes 33 of the most commonly used and needed bolt extractors, covering sizes from 6mm to 3/4". It is suitable for tasks like automotive repairs, home renovations, dismantling auto parts, and machine maintenance.
*   Versatile Tool Compatibility: Our extractor set is equipped with adapters and square head sockets that fit 3/8" and 1/4" manual or power tools, such as adjustable wrenches, ratchets, and electric wrenches. You can perform disassembly tasks using the tools you already have without the need for additional accessories or tools.
*   Cr-Mo Bolt Extractors: The broken bolt extractors are made from high-hardness, robust Cr-Mo steel material, and they are resistant to breakage. The sandblasted surface enhances wear resistance and rust resistance. The extractors are laser-engraved with sizes for easy identification and quick selection.
*   Non-Slip Design: The stripped bolt remover is designed with a reverse spiral for a secure grip on threads, especially when counterclockwise disassembling, ensuring a tight connection. The hexagonal top design prevents wrenches or sockets from slipping off, making bolt removal efficient and effortless.
*   Waterproof Storage Case: The nut extractor set comes with a waterproof PP case, providing organized storage for your tools. It's easy to carry anywhere and ensures your tools are neatly stored, preventing them from getting lost, rusting, or getting damaged.

## Product Description

 Specifications Item Model Number: YM-29 Material: Cr-Mo Net Weight: 4.2 lbs/1.9 kg Dimensions: 9.3 x 6.3 x 2.2 inch/235 x 160 x 55 mm Package Content 29 x Bolt Extractor 1 x Checklist 1 x 3/8" Square to 1/4" Hex Adapter 1 x 1/4" Square to 1/4" Hex Adapter 1 x 3/8" to 1/4" Square Socket 1 x 1/4" to 3/8" Square Socket
